# Function: Car\_LocationList

* * *

## 1 Overview

This service allows the customer to search for all car rental stations in a requested geographical area. The service can be applied in two different modes:

**1) IATA Car Location List**

The IATA Car Location List is used to retrieve a list of all car rental stations in a given city, airport or country, represented by an IATA code.  For a city or an airport location, the results list can be retrieved for either all or for a particular car provider. At country level, the results list can only be retrieved for a particular car provider.

**2) POR Car Location List**

A Point Of Reference (POR) is a geographical point that represents any type of location that is of interest to the user (for example, hotel, theatre, zoo, etc.). A POR can be represented in two different ways:

-   longitude and latitude information
-   POR Business ID.  This is a unique ID assigned to a POR when it is geocoded on the POR database. It consists of the POR Category Code and the POR Foreign Key. The POR Category Code represents the category of the POR, for example CAR for car rental  location or ATT for an attraction. The POR Foreign Key is a unique identifier that is internally generated when a location is added to the POR database.

The user can search for all car rental stations within a given radius around a given POR. The results list can be retrieved for either all or for a particular car provider.

## 1.1 Supported Operations

The service can be used to retrieve a list of car locations by searching over the following criteria:

\- by single provider

\- by all providers

\- by seamless providers

\- by non-seamless providers

\- by specific services - collection , delivery , out of hours

\- by country/state (for a specific provider)

\- by airport

\- by city with vicinity code

\- by poiint of reference with geocodes and radius

\- by point of reference with POR Business ID

## 1.2 Limitations

none

## 1.3 Unsupported Operations

none

## 1.4 Prerequisites

none

## 2 Building A Query

Mandatory parameters

The mandatory information needed to build a location list request is that defining the geographical location. Depending on the request reference (airport, city, country, point of interest ), different input parameters are mandatory.

The two request modes, IATA and POR location lists, are mutually exclusive; that is, the input parameters of the two modes cannot be included within the same request.  
  
**1) IATA Car Location List**

-   the 3 letter IATA city or airport code
-   the 2 letter IATA country code (or state for the countries that have states), vicinity identification code and car provider code

**2) POR Car Location List**

-   the POR represented by its longitude and latitude
-   the POR represented by its POR Business ID, that is, POR Category Code + POR Foreign Key

If both POR identifiers - longitude/latitude and POR Business ID - are provided within the same request, the POR Business ID has priority.  
  
Optional  Parameters  
  
1) Vicinity code

2) Car company code

3) Service parameter

4) Search radius and radius unit (only for POR location list)

5) Seamless/non-seamless car provider

6) Scrolling

## 2.1 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Country / Worldwide

## 2.1.1 Description

The 2-letter IATA country code (or state for countries that have states) or for a full list location the value 'WWD' for WorldWide.

## 2.1.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<countryState> <countryCode>FR</countryCode> </countryState>

## 2.2 Sub Structure: Mandatory: IATA Airport or City

## 2.2.1 Description

The 3-letter IATA city or airport  code, and the specific identifier for the selected type:

**26**    for city  
**215**  for airport

## 2.2.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>26</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>MRS</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection>

## 2.3 Sub Structure: Mandatory: POR Business ID

## 2.3.1 Description

The POR Business ID is the POR Category Code + POR Foreign Key.

Each car rental location, when added to the Amadeus system by the car provider, receives an automatically generated value for the Business ID. This value is internal to Amadeus and can be used only if determined previously (provider as a list or extracted from a previous location list response). For more accurate results, this search can also contain a distance for the search radius.

The POR category code can be :

APT - AIRPORT  
ATT - ATTRACTION  
BST - BUS STATION  
CAR - CAR RENTAL LOCATION  
CNV - CONVENTION CENTER  
CTY - CITY  
EDU - EDUCATIONAL  
EVT - EVENT  
GRD - GROUND TRANSPORT  
HEL - HELIPORT  
HLE - HOTEL LEISURE  
HTL - HOTEL  
MIL - MILITARY BASES  
PAR - PARK / RECREATIONAL AREA  
PRT - PORT  
RAI - RAIL STATION  
RST - RESORTS / SKI AREA  
SHO - SHOPPING CENTER  
SPO - SPORTS  
TRA - TRANSPORTATION POINTS

## 2.3.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<geoCoding> <dummy></dummy> <porBusinessID> <porCategoryCode> <categoryCodeInfo> <categoryCode>CAR</categoryCode> </categoryCodeInfo> </porCategoryCode> <porForeignKey> <referenceDetails> <value>192052</value> </referenceDetails> </porForeignKey> </porBusinessID> </geoCoding>

## 2.4 Sub Structure: Mandatory: POR Geocode

## 2.4.1 Description

The longitude and latitude coordinates of a POR, in following format:

longitude

number of 1/100000 degrees east of Greenwich (positive values) or west of Greenwich (negative values)  

latitude

number of 1/100000 degrees north of the equator (positive values) or south of the equator (negative values)

For example, the Eiffel Tower in Paris, France, has longitude 229481 and latitude 4885760.

## 2.4.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<geoCoding> <dummy></dummy> <position> <encoding>POR</encoding> <porPosition> <porLongitude>229481</porLongitude> <porLatitude>4885760</porLatitude> </porPosition> </position> </geoCoding>

## 2.5 Sub Structure: Optional: Car Provider Code

## 2.5.1 Description

The 2-letter car provider code can be specified in the location list request.

A maximum of five car provider codes can be specified; therefore, the locations for a maximum of five providers can be obtained in one response.

If nothing is specified, all provider locations are returned.

## 2.5.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<companySelection> <companyInformation> <companyCode>ZI</companyCode> </companyInformation> </companySelection>

## 2.6 Sub Structure: Optional: Radius and Radius Unit

## 2.6.1 Description

For the geocode search, a radius distance and unit can be specified . If no value is entered for either radius or unit, a radius of 5km is used as default.

Radius unit by unit code according to OTA Unit of Measure code:

1

Miles

2

Kilometers

## 2.6.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<distance> <measurementQualifier>DST</measurementQualifier> <measurementDetails> <significance>FLY</significance> </measurementDetails> <valueRange> <measureUnitQualifier>2</measureUnitQualifier> <measurementValue>10</measurementValue> </valueRange> </distance>

## 2.7 Sub Structure: Optional: Scrolling

## 2.7.1 Description

The location list response can include up to 600 locations. If only a limited number of locations is required, this can be specified in the request.

The user can specify the number of the first location and the total number of locations to be displayed. This last number can be a positive value to scroll down or a negative value to scroll up. If the user requests items that are outside the list of items, then the error message OUT OF RANGE is returned.

## 2.7.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<scrollingInfo> <scrollingInformation> <numberOfItems>1</numberOfItems> <lastItemRetrieved>200</lastItemRetrieved> </scrollingInformation> </scrollingInfo>

## 2.8 Sub Structure: Optional: Seamless Indicator

## 2.8.1 Description

You can restrict the search to seamless car providers or to non-seamless car providers:

-   Seamless car providers are those for which the location data is retrieved directly from the provider system (except for geocode data, which is taken from the Amadeus POR system).

-   Non-seamless car providers are those for which the location data is retrieved from the Amadeus database, where it was entered and is maintained by the car provider.

**Code Set**

**Description**

NSL

only non-seamless providers

SL

only seamless providers

## 2.8.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<seamlessIndicator> <statusInformation> <indicator>NSL</indicator> </statusInformation> </seamlessIndicator>

## 2.9 Sub Structure: Optional: Service Parameter

## 2.9.1 Description

Car providers enter in the Amadeus DB the supported services for each of their car rental stations.  A request with service codes returns only car locations that offer the requested services:

**Code**

**Description**

COL

Collection service

DEL

Delivery service

OHR

Out-of-office hours service

## 2.9.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<selectionOptions> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> </accessTypeInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>OHR</vicinityIndicator> </vicinityInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>COL</vicinityIndicator> </vicinityInfo> </selectionOptions>

## 2.10 Sub Structure: Optional: Vicinity Code

## 2.10.1 Description

A request with a vicinity code returns only car rental stations with the given vicinity code. The vicinity code can have the following values:

**Code**

**Description**

A

Airport area

T

Airport terminal

O

Off-airport terminal

C

City centre

N

North of city

E

East of city

W

West of city

S

South of city

R

Resort

H

Hotel

P

Port/ferry

X

Rail

  D

Downtown area (all except airport area)

## 2.10.2 Xml Structure

* * *

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<selectionOptions> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> </accessTypeInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>VIC</vicinityIndicator> <vicinityCode>C</vicinityCode> </vicinityInfo> </selectionOptions>

## 3 Receiving A Reply

A successful location list response contains car provider locations that match the search criteria.

The information that is always returned in the response is the following:

\- provider code

\- scrolling info

\- location address

\- gecoding details

\- service parameters and car company access indicator

In addition, depending on the information requested, the following information is returned :

\- distance from POI for each location returned

\- country code, city/airport info and alternative cities

Some information is returned for each location in the list (service, geocodes, address, distance) while other information is present in the generic part , at the beginning of the response (scrolling, country data).

Moreover, results ordering is done as follows:

-   For POI search: The sequence in which car rental stations are returned is sorted by their distance (ascending order) to the search point.

-   For the other types of search, the results are ordered by car provider, first alphabetically and then by vicinity (off terminal , terminal , city etc.) and vicinity number.

Exceptions:

-   If particular car providers are requested, the sequence is sorted by the order in which the car providers are requested.
-   If a car provider is a seamless car location provider, this provider is listed on top of the list.

## 3.1 Sub Structure: Manadatory: Scrolling Info

## 3.1.1 Description

Even if no scrolling parameters were specified in the request, the location list response always includes the total number of locations in the response.

This information is returned at generic level.

## 3.1.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<scrollingInfo> <scrollingInformation> <numberOfItems>0</numberOfItems> <lastItemRetrieved>94</lastItemRetrieved> </scrollingInformation> </scrollingInfo>

* * *

## 3.2 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Geocoding Details

## 3.2.1 Description

For each of the locations returned in the location list response, geocodes are included, as well as the accuracy of the geocodes.

The information is returned at location\-specific level.

The geocode accuracy is indicated by the following possible values:

**Code**

**Description**

110

Manual (car rental location was manually geocoded)

100

Address level

90

Intersection level

80

Street level

70

ZIP 9 (US only) level

60

ZIP 7 (US only) level

50

Zip (or postal) level

40

City level

30

County level

20

State level

10

Country level

1

Geocoding Failed

0

Unscaled

  2

  Ambigouos geocodes

The best quality level is 110 (manual) and 100 (address level); the lowest quality level is 10 (country level) or 1 , 2 and 0.

Also in this section, the POR Business ID - an  internal Amadeus value - and the POR category code are included. See the full list of categories in the Building a Request section.

## 3.2.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<geoCoding> <dummy></dummy> <position> <encoding>POR</encoding> <porPosition> <porLongitude>519086</porLongitude> <porLatitude>4339331</porLatitude> </porPosition> </position> <porBusinessID> <porCategoryCode> <categoryCodeInfo> <categoryCode>CAR</categoryCode> </categoryCodeInfo> </porCategoryCode> <porForeignKey> <referenceDetails> <value>179114</value> </referenceDetails> </porForeignKey> </porBusinessID> <porAccuracy> <statusCode>80</statusCode> </porAccuracy> </geoCoding>

* * *

## 3.3 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Location Address

## 3.3.1 Description

The address of the car rental location - as defined by the car provider in the Amadeus database - is returned in the location list response.

This information is returned at location\-specific level.

The address usually includes street, number, postal code, city and the car company's Amadeus location code, as defined in the Amadeus database.

In case of a worldwide provider location list the address must also contains the country or state.

## 3.3.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<locationAddress> <addressInfo> <addressFormat>003</addressFormat> <address>MARSEILLE AEROPORT BP 55 AP DE MARSEILLE, P 11</address> </addressInfo> <cityName>MARIGNANE CEDEX</cityName> <locationInformation> <locationCode>MRST01</locationCode> <agency>CPY</agency> </locationInformation> </locationAddress>

* * *

## 3.4 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Provider Data

## 3.4.1 Description

For each of the locations returned in the location list response, the provider code and name are included.

This information is returned at location\-specific level.

## 3.4.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<companyInfo> <companyInformation> <companyCode>SX</companyCode> <companyName>SIXT</companyName> </companyInformation> </companyInfo>

* * *

## 3.5 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Service Parameters

## 3.5.1 Description

If the car location supports additional services, such as delivery, collection, out of hours pickup and shuttle service, these are indicated in in the location list response. If the car company does not support these services, the indicators are not returned.

The information is returned at location specific level.

Also in the response is the car company access indicator, which indicates the type of connectivity the car provider has.  Currently all car providers are Complete Access Plus (CP).

**Code**

**Description**

CP

Complete Access Plus

CA

Complete Access

SA

Standard Access

For the shuttle service, the following values can be returned:

AT

Take airport-provided transportation to rental counter and vehicle

CA

Call rental company for pick up

IN

Rental counter in terminal and walk to vehicle

MS

Take airport-provided transportation to a drop point and then additional shuttle to rental counter and vehicle

NA

Take shuttle to rental counter and vehicle, no information provided on type of shuttle

OF

Rental company shuttle to off airport location

RC

Take rental company provided shuttle to rental counter and vehicle

SV

Rental counter in terminal, shuttle to vehicle

T

Terminal

## 3.5.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<companyAccessType> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> <accessType>CP</accessType> </accessTypeInfo> <otherSelectionDetails> <option>COL</option> </otherSelectionDetails> <otherSelectionDetails> <option>DEL</option> </otherSelectionDetails> <otherSelectionDetails> <option>SHT</option> <optionInformation>IN</optionInformation> </otherSelectionDetails> </companyAccessType>

* * *

## 3.6 Sub Structure: Mandatory: Structured Opening Hours

## 3.6.1 Description

The weekday opening hours of each location is returned in the location list response.

This information is taken from the Amadeus database, in which providers have previously entered the opening hours for each of their car rental locations.

The response contains the opening hours for each day, a DOW indicator specifying that the day of the week follows, followed by the codified day of the week:

code

Day of the week

1

Monday

2

Tuesday

3

Wednesday

4

Thursday

5

Friday

6

Saturday

7

Sunday

For each day, a maximum of 2 repetitions is allowed, therefore a break can exist for each day.

## 3.7 Sub Structure: Optional: Airport and Terminal

## 3.7.1 Description

For airport terminal locations, additional information regarding the airport code and terminal is returned for each location.

This information is taken from the Amadeus database and has been previously supplied by the car provider.

Note that a car rental location can have more than one terminal returned, as providers are allowed to associate more than one terminal to one rental location.

## 3.7.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<airportTerminalInfo> <airportCode>CDG</airportCode> <terminalInformation> <name>TERMINAL2A</name> </terminalInformation> </airportTerminalInfo>

* * *

## 3.8 Sub Structure: Optional: Associated locations when IATA code changes

## 3.8.1 Description

Whenever an IATA city or airport code changes, both Amadeus and car providers get notified:

-   Amadeus performs referencial data changes in its database
-   Car providers migrate their car locations stored in Amadeus database to adapt the IATA code changes.

During this period where all car providers are notified to update their locations, it might happen that some of the car providers already updated the location, but some of them havn't. As a result, in Web service channel, customer couldn't find all locations they want.

Amadeus developped the functionality to solve this issue, to return all locations associated to the query during the migration period.

This means that for Web service channel, different locations with extra content could be returned during this phase.

Following is an example about the migration phase of Augsburg (AGB) city and airport:

IATA request to update airport Augsburg (code AGB), previously attached to city Augsburg (AGB), now to become attached to city Munich (MUC). City Augsburg (code AGB) is decided to be removed by IATA.

The request to AGB city returns results of AGB airport locations + AGB city locations (as normal process) and additionally MUC city locations + MUC city locations.

The request to MUC city returns results of MUC city locations + MUC city locations (as normal process) and additionally AGB city locations + AGB city locations.

Same logic applies when request AGB/MUC airport.

* * *

## 3.9 Sub Structure: Optional: Country, City and Alternative Cities

## 3.9.1 Description

If the request was a search by IATA airport or city code, the location country, city and alternative cities are returned in the response. This is not the case in the POR search.

The information is returned at a generic level.

Alternative cities are returned in the response only if alternative cities exist for the IATA city code for any of the car providers represented in the response.

## 3.9.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<carLocationListDetails> <countryAndState> <countryCode>FR</countryCode> </countryAndState> <cityAndAssociated> <cityOrAirportTag>026</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>NCE</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAndAssociated> <cityAndAssociated> <cityOrAirportTag>026</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>NCE</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> <associatedCityInfo> <associatedCityCode>XAT</associatedCityCode> </associatedCityInfo> </cityAndAssociated> </carLocationListDetails>

* * *

## 3.10 Sub Structure: Optional: Distance from POI

## 3.10.1 Description

If the request was a search by POI, the location list response includes the distance from each car location to the requested POI. If no radius unit was specified in the request, the value used in the response is km.

If the request was by airport location, the response contains the distance between each off-airport location returned and the airport specified.

The information is returned at location specific level.

## 3.10.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<geoCoding> <distance> <measurementQualifier>DST</measurementQualifier> <measurementDetails> <significance>FLY</significance> </measurementDetails> <valueRange> <measureUnitQualifier>2</measureUnitQualifier> <measurementValue>2.6</measurementValue> </valueRange> </distance> </geoCoding>

* * *

## 3.11 Sub Structure: Optional: Phone numbers and E-mail Address

## 3.11.1 Description

When requested the Location List can expose, in the reply, Phone number, Fax and e-mail address always with a possibility to have the type and purpose of the data. The purpose will allocated at segement PHE, data element "contactLocation", where customer shall contact.

Code

Type of contact

PH1

Phone number

P01

Fax number

P02

E-mail address

## 3.11.2 Xml Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<carLocationListDetails> <phoneAndEmail> <phoneOrEmailType>PH1</phoneOrEmailType> <contactLocation>SUPPORT</contactLocation> <telephoneNumberDetails> <internationalDialCode>01</internationalDialCode> <localPrefix>44</localPrefix> <areaCode>55</areaCode> <telephoneNumber>55555555</telephoneNumber> </telephoneNumberDetails> <emailAddress>provider@provider.com</emailAddress> </phoneAndEmail> </carLocationListDetails>

* * *

## 4 Error Messages

The list of possible error messages that can be returned in the location list response is the following:

-   EN 23378: INVALID SEARCH RADIUS
-   EN 23379: INVALID SEARCH RADIUS UNIT
-   EN 23380: NO CAR RENTAL LOC FOUND FOR REQ AREA
-   EN 32614: PLEASE ENTER VALID GEOCODES(IN DECIMAL DEGREES)
-   EN 23376: INVALID GEOGRAPHICAL DATA FORMAT
-   EN 23377: INVALID POR ID
-   EN 10426: INVALID CAR COMPANY CODE
-   EN 23382: NOTHING FOUND FOR REQ AREA
-   EN 23381: POINT OF REFERENCE SERVER TEMPORARILY NOT AVAILABLE
-   EN 558    : LOCATION NOT IN SYSTEM
-   EN 458    : INVALID OR MISSING - CMPY CODE
-   EN 9004  : NOTHING FOUND FOR REQ COUNTRY
-   EN 896    : NOTHING FOUND FOR REQ STATE
-   EN 9005  : NOTHING FOUND FOR REQ CITY
-   EN 898   : STATE CODE REQUIRED FOR US
-   EN 418   : CAR COMPANY NOT AT LOCATION

  

* * *

## 5 Operations

## 5.1 Operation: Car location list for all providers

This operation shows a location list request for all car providers. (When no provider is specified in the request, locations for all providers are returned.)

In this example, the location list request takes the following parameters:

-   Car Provider: all (none specified)
-   Location: Marseille city

## 5.1.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_22\_4\_1A"> <cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>26</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>MRS</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.1.3 Possible Errors

See the "Error Messages" section.

* * *

## 5.2 Operation: Car location list for a city with vicinity

This operation requests a location list for all car providers in Paris city centre.

In this example, the location list request takes the following parameters:

-   Car Provider: All (nothing specified)
-   Location: PAR (Paris city)
-   Vicinity code: C (city centre)

All provider locations in Paris with the vicinity C are returned in the response.

## 5.2.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>215</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>PAR</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection> <selectionOptions> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> </accessTypeInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>VIC</vicinityIndicator> <vicinityCode>C</vicinityCode> </vicinityInfo> </selectionOptions> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.3 Operation: Car location list for a list of providers, for city locations near railway stations

This operation shows a car location list request for a list of specified car providers (maximum five) in Nice near railway stations.

This example takes the following options:

-   Car providers: ZD/ZI/ZE/EP/SX
-   City : Nice
-   Vicinity code : X (for railway station)

All the car locations for the specified providers located near railway stations in Nice are returned in the response.

## 5.4 Operation: Car location list for a provider worldwide

1.  This operation returns the full list of locations for a car provider. This is done by specifying worldwide value instead of a country IATA code and not specifying any vicinity.

Because of the large amount of result, the scrolling feature of the message must be used

In the example, the request takes the following parameters:

-   Provider: SX (Sixt)
-   County: WWD (WorldWide)
-   First record position in the list to return: 5
-   Number of records to return: 20

All Sixt locations between records 5 and 24 are returned in the response (in the XML reply exemple, only few locations are shown here because it would be too heavy otherwise)

In response we can see that there is still 272 locations to retrieve.

Request to retrieve them would be then

-   Provider: SX (Sixt)
-   County: WWD (WorldWide)
-   First record position in the list to return: 25
-   Number of records to return: 272

## 5.5 Operation: Car location list for a single provider in a city

This operation requests a location list for a single car provider in Nice.

In this example, the location list request takes following parameters:

-   Car Provider: EP
-   Location : NCE city (code 26)

## 5.6.3 Possible Errors

See the "Error Messages" section.

* * *

## 5.7 Operation: Car location list for country

This operation returns a list of locations for a car provider for a specific county. This is done by specifying a vicinity in the request (if no vicinity is specified, the request returns an error).

In the example, the request takes the following parameters:

-   Provider: EP (Europcar)
-   County: FR (France)
-   Vicinity code: C (city centre)  
    

All Europcar locations in France with the vicinity code V are returned in the response.  

## 5.7.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <companySelection> <companyInformation> <companyCode>EP</companyCode> </companyInformation> </companySelection> <countryState> <countryCode>FR</countryCode> </countryState> <selectionOptions> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> </accessTypeInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>VIC</vicinityIndicator> <vicinityCode>C</vicinityCode> </vicinityInfo> </selectionOptions> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.8 Operation: Car location list for seamless providers

This operation returns a location list for all  seamless providers.

In this example, the location list request takes the following options:

-   Car Provider: all (none specified)
-   Location: CDG airport
-   Seamless provider indicator: SL

Only SX locations are returned in the response as they are a seamless provider.

## 5.8.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>215</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>CDG</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection> <seamlessIndicator> <statusInformation> <indicator>SL</indicator> </statusInformation> </seamlessIndicator> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.8.2 Reply Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationListReply xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRR\_13\_1\_1A"> <scrollingInfo> <scrollingInformation> <numberOfItems>0</numberOfItems> <lastItemRetrieved>1</lastItemRetrieved> </scrollingInformation> </scrollingInfo> <countryAndState> <countryCode>FR</countryCode> </countryAndState> <cityAndAssociated> <cityOrAirportTag>026</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>PAR</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAndAssociated> <carLocationListDetails> <companyInfo> <companyInformation> <companyCode>SX</companyCode> <companyName>SIXT</companyName> </companyInformation> </companyInfo> <locationAddress> <addressInfo> <addressFormat>003</addressFormat> <address>PARIS CHARLES DE GAULLE AEROP TERMINAL 1</address> </addressInfo> <cityName> 2</cityName> <postalCode>PARIS ROISSY</postalCode> <locationInformation> <locationCode>PART01</locationCode> <agency>CPY</agency> </locationInformation> </locationAddress> <companyAccessType> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> <accessType>CP</accessType> </accessTypeInfo> <otherSelectionDetails> <option>SHT</option> <optionInformation>IN</optionInformation> </otherSelectionDetails> </companyAccessType> <geoCoding> <dummy></dummy> <position> <encoding>POR</encoding> <porPosition> <porLongitude>254125</porLongitude> <porLatitude>4901425</porLatitude> </porPosition> </position> <porBusinessID> <porCategoryCode> <categoryCodeInfo> <categoryCode>CAR</categoryCode> </categoryCodeInfo> </porCategoryCode> <porForeignKey> <referenceDetails> <value>6361</value> </referenceDetails> </porForeignKey> </porBusinessID> <porAccuracy> <statusCode>40</statusCode> </porAccuracy> </geoCoding> </carLocationListDetails> </Car\_LocationListReply>

## 5.8.3 Possible Errors

See the "Error Messages" section.

* * *

## 5.9 Operation: Car location list with error returned

This operation shows a location list request for a location in which a specific car provider is not present.  An error is returned.

In this example, the location list request takes the following options:

-   Car Provider: Enterprise
-   Location:  Timisoara (TSR)

No location exists for Enterprise for this IATA code, therefore the following error message is returned: CAR COMPANY NOT AT LOCATION.

## 5.9.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <companySelection> <companyInformation> <companyCode>ET</companyCode> </companyInformation> </companySelection> <cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>26</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>TSR</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.9.2 Reply Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationListReply xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRR\_13\_1\_1A"> <errorDetails> <errorCode> <applicationErrorDetail> <errorNumber>418</errorNumber> <responseType>EC</responseType> <responsibleAgency>1A</responsibleAgency> </applicationErrorDetail> </errorCode> <errorInformation> <freeTextDetails> <freeTextQualifier>003</freeTextQualifier> </freeTextDetails> <freeText>CAR COMPANY NOT AT LOCATION</freeText> </errorInformation> </errorDetails> </Car\_LocationListReply>

## 5.9.3 Possible Errors

See the "Error Messages" section.

* * *

## 5.10 Operation: Car location list with services

This operation shows a request for car rental locations that offer a specific service.  It requests only locations that support out of office hours (delivery and collection services can also be requested in this way).

The request takes the following parameters:

-   Provider : ALL (none is requested)
-   Location : city Miami
-   Service supported : OHR (out of office hours)

The response contains all the locations that have the OHR service available for Miami.

## 5.10.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <cityAirportSelection> <cityOrAirportTag>26</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>MIA</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> </cityAirportSelection> <selectionOptions> <accessTypeInfo> <companyAccessIndicator>CAT</companyAccessIndicator> </accessTypeInfo> <vicinityInfo> <vicinityIndicator>OHR</vicinityIndicator> </vicinityInfo> </selectionOptions> </Car\_LocationList>

## 5.10.2 Reply Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

</scrollingInformation> </scrollingInfo> <countryAndState> <countryCode>US</countryCode> <state> <stateCode>FL</stateCode> </state> </countryAndState> <cityAndAssociated> <cityAndAssociated> <cityOrAirportTag>026</cityOrAirportTag> <locationInfo> <airportOrCityCode>MIA</airportOrCityCode> </locationInfo> <associatedCityInfo> <associatedCityCode>HST</associatedCityCode> ## 5.11 Operation: POR Car location list with longitude, latitude

This operation returns a list of car rental offices around a POR with longitude '727891', latitude '4370143' and 10 km search radius. All the car rental locations in the search area are returned with their details.

## 5.11.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

* * *

## 5.12 Operation: POR Car location list with POR Business ID

If the customer has access to the POR Business IDs of rental locations, a request can be sent to retrieve the rental locations around a point of reference.

In the example, the request contains the following parameters:

-   Search Radius: 5 km
-   POR Business ID: 130029
-   POR category: ATT (attraction)

All the car rental locations within 5 km of the search point are returned in the response.

## 5.12.1 Query Structure

![](/resources/externalFiles/images/userguideeditor/icon_collapse.gif)XML Example

<Car\_LocationList xmlns="http://xml.amadeus.com/RCLLRQ\_13\_1\_1A"> <geoCoding> <dummy></dummy> <distance> <measurementQualifier>DST</measurementQualifier> <measurementDetails> <significance>FLY</significance> </measurementDetails> <valueRange> <measureUnitQualifier>2</measureUnitQualifier> <measurementValue>5</measurementValue> </valueRange> </distance> <porBusinessID> <porCategoryCode> <categoryCodeInfo> <categoryCode>CAR</categoryCode> </categoryCodeInfo> </porCategoryCode> <porForeignKey> <referenceDetails> <value>130029</value> </referenceDetails> </porForeignKey> </porBusinessID> </geoCoding> </Car\_LocationList>
